        Idea:
vibecomfy: validator-pre-submit-gate v1 — promote the existing schema validator into a hard pre-submit gate that runs against the live runtime's `/object_info`, fail loudly with actionable messages, and add the missing checks (link shape, range/enum) that let real bugs through. Do NOT rewrite the materializer.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem

Several "HiddenSwitch incompatibilities" in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` are actually our own materializer/harness bugs that submit invalid graphs the runtime then chokes on:

- ACE materializer emitted dict-shaped links instead of standard Comfy link arrays (`KeyError: 0` in `_compress_graph_nodes`).
- ACE got `bpm=2` (declared min is 10) because we trusted UI widget positions; missing required API fields (`top_p`, `min_p`, `top_k`, `temperature`).
- Both classes are submit-invalid by the runtime's own `/object_info` schema and could be caught up-front.

The infrastructure to catch these already exists but is not wired:

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)` checks unknown class_types, missing required inputs, unknown inputs, edge type mismatches.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py` — `SchemaProvider` fetches `/object_info` and caches it under `vibecomfy/schema/cache.py:object_info_cache_path`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:36` — `ComfyClient.object_info()` already exists.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `VibeWorkflow.validate(schema_provider=None)` exists but the provider parameter is **optional and defaults to None**, so schema checks no-op.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:_prepare_prompt()` (around line 327) calls `workflow.validate()` **with no provider**. The comment explicitly says "Runtime submissions stay structural-only to avoid schema lookup cost on every invocation." — that comment justifies the bug. Caching solves the cost concern.

The validator also misses two checks that would have caught real bugs:
- **Range/enum**: ACE `bpm=2` slipped through because numeric ranges are not enforced.
- **Link shape**: dict-shaped links in API JSON pass structural checks but break the runtime; there is no API-shape sanity check.

# Goal

Make schema validation a hard pre-submit gate. Five mechanical steps:

1. Wire a SchemaProvider into `_prepare_prompt()` and gate submission on `validate(schema_provider=...)`.
2. Add range/enum enforcement to `validate_against_schema`.
3. Add API-link-shape enforcement (every connected input must be `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`, not a dict).
4. Add an opt-out list (`SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`) for known-lying custom-node schemas, with each entry pointing to a `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` row.
5. Make failure messages name the node, class_type, input, and the violated rule — and surface them via the existing CLI surfaces (`vibecomfy run`, `vibecomfy validate`).

Materializer rewrite is explicitly OUT of scope. This is a validation gate only.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — entry point `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:122` — SchemaProvider caches per-server-fingerprint at `object_info_cache_path()`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:148` — first-call fetch is async via `object_info_async()`; subsequent calls are cache-hits.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `validate()` already returns `ValidationReport(ok, issues)`; `ok` is False if any issue has `severity="error"`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` `_prepare_prompt()` already raises `ValueError("Workflow validation failed: ...")` on `not report.ok`. Wiring a provider is sufficient to turn schema checks on.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:20` — `queue_prompt()` posts to `/prompt`; this is the submit boundary.
- `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py` exists as a CLI surface.

# Steps

## S1: Wire SchemaProvider into the submit gate

- In `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`:
  - Construct or accept a `SchemaProvider` for the active runtime URL once per session (not per submit). Store it on the session/runner.
  - Pass it into `workflow.validate(schema_provider=provider)` inside `_prepare_prompt()`.
  - Update the comment that currently justifies skipping schema validation; note that caching makes per-submit cost negligible.
- Default behavior: if `/object_info` cannot be fetched and no cache exists (e.g. fully offline materialization-only path), fall back to structural-only validation with a single WARNING line printed once per session. Never silently skip; never hard-fail when the provider itself is unavailable. Hard-fail only on actual schema violations.

## S2: Range and enum enforcement

- In `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:validate_against_schema`, after the missing/unknown input checks:
  - For each provided input whose schema spec declares numeric `min`/`max`, compare the supplied value (after coercion) and emit `ValidationIssue("value_out_of_range", ...)` on violation.
  - For each input whose schema spec declares an enum/options list, emit `ValidationIssue("value_not_in_enum", ...)` if the value is not a member.
  - Both at `severity="error"`.
- Inspect the existing schema-loading path in `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:_schema_from_object_info` to confirm the spec shape (it likely already carries `min`/`max`/`options` from the raw `/object_info`); if not, extend it minimally to surface them.

## S3: API link-shape enforcement

- Add a new check (can live in `validate_against_schema` or a sibling `validate_api_shape` called from `VibeWorkflow.validate`):
  - For every input on every node, after compilation/edge resolution, the value must be one of: a primitive scalar, a list/tuple, a dict (only when the schema declares dict-shaped input), or an API link in the form `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`.
  - Specifically detect and reject dict-shaped links such as `{"link": ..., "node": ...}` connected to inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Emit `ValidationIssue("invalid_link_shape", ...)`.
- This check runs against the compiled API dict from `VibeWorkflow.compile(backend="api")` if that's the cleanest place; otherwise pre-compile inside `validate()`.

## S4: Per-class skip list for known-lying custom-node schemas

- New module-level constant in `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py`: `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES: dict[str, str]` mapping `class_type` → human-readable reason (and ideally a URL/anchor into `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md`).
- For each class_type in the skip list, suppress only `unknown_input` and `value_*` issues — still enforce `unknown_class_type` and `missing_required_input` (those are usually correct even when widget schemas are wrong).
- Initial entries: empty. Add the first entry only when a real workflow trips a check that is provably the schema's fault, not the workflow's, and add a note in the incompatibilities doc with `Root cause: custom_node_contract`.

## S5: CLI and reporting

- `vibecomfy validate <workflow>` (in `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py`): make schema validation default-on, with `--no-schema` to fall back to structural-only.
- `vibecomfy run <workflow>` and `vibecomfy session ...` paths: the gate is implicit (S1); on failure, print every issue with node id, class_type, input, and the human-readable rule violated, then exit nonzero. Do not print only the first issue.
- Update existing tests under `tests/` that currently submit invalid graphs (if any) to either fix the graph or explicitly call validate-with-skip.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)

- Materializer rewrite. The materializer keeps its current shape; the validator catches its bugs at the boundary. A separate brief will revisit the materializer if the validator surfaces a recurring class of materializer bugs (≥3 times across different workflows).
- Pre-flight doctor that bundles validator + node-presence + model-presence + preview-stripping into a single gating report. Defer.
- Per-workflow input contract / fixture declaration system. Separate brief.
- Auto-repair: the validator only diagnoses, never mutates the graph.
- Type-coercion changes (string vs int widget values). Validator should accept whatever the runtime accepts.

# Design constraints

- **Reversibility**: every behavior change must be toggleable via a single env var or flag (`VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` to disable, or `--no-schema` on the CLI). Do not make the gate non-bypassable.
- **Cache hits**: schema validation must be a cache-hit on every submit after the first per-runtime; first-fetch latency is acceptable.
- **No new top-level deps**.
- **Failure messages must be actionable**: every error names the node id, class_type, the input or rule violated, and the value (truncated if large). No bare "schema check failed".
- **Skip list discipline**: every entry in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` must be cross-referenced from `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` per the Contributing rules in that doc.
- **Do not gate non-submit code paths**: `vibecomfy convert`, `vibecomfy materialize`, etc. should not trigger the gate. Only paths that actually submit to `/prompt` enforce it.

# No prereq clauses

This brief has no hard-halt prereqs. It is independent of the model-registry brief (#5), the watchdog subagent (#2), the override-deletion subagent (#3), and the fixture-library subagent (#4). Ship as soon as ready; do not wait on any of them.

# Validation evidence to produce

- Re-run the previously-failing ACE materialization (the dict-shaped link case and the bpm=2 case): both must now hard-fail at submit with messages that name the node, the class_type, and the rule.
- Re-run the existing 14 runtime-green workflows: all must still pass the gate (no regressions).
- Add unit tests in `tests/test_schema_validate.py` for: missing required input, unknown input, value-out-of-range, value-not-in-enum, invalid-link-shape, skip-list suppression, structural-only fallback when provider is unavailable.

# Open questions

- Should the gate run on the structural workflow object or on the compiled API dict? Both shapes have different bug surfaces (link shape only matters in API form). Lean toward: structural checks on the object, link-shape checks on the compiled API. Validator returns a single combined report.
- Should `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` live in code or in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` as a parsed table? V1: code constant with a docstring pointer to the doc. Parsed-from-doc is a follow-up.
- Cache invalidation: what happens when the runtime upgrades and `/object_info` shape changes? The current cache is fingerprinted by `runtime_fingerprint(server_url)`; confirm that fingerprint includes a runtime version or build hash. If not, that's a separate bug worth flagging but not blocking this brief.

  "watch_items": [
    "Async warmup correctness (FLAG-001): `_prepare_prompt_async` MUST `await provider.object_info_async()` to populate `_object_info` BEFORE calling sync `workflow.validate(schema_provider=...)`. If you call `provider.object_info()` (sync) from inside an async context, `_run_async` raises RuntimeError and the gate silently degrades to structural-only \u2014 the exact bug the gate flagged. Verify with `test_async_warmup_populates_cache_then_validates`.",
    "Submit-order invariant (FLAG-002): the runtime/comfy_server must exist BEFORE the schema provider is built. ServerSession.run was previously calling `_prepare_prompt` before `await self.start()`; runtime/run.py was calling it before entering `comfy_server`. Both reorders are mandatory \u2014 validating against `None` or a different URL than `/prompt` defeats the gate. Verify with `test_server_session_validates_against_started_url` and `test_one_shot_run_validates_against_active_url`.",
    "Embedded path must NOT spawn an extra `comfy_server`: an embedded `Comfy` is already in-process. Use `cache_only=True` and `load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)` directly.     "WARNING-once discipline: the `_schema_warning_emitted` flag (or per-invocation `warned` closure in runtime/run.py) must ensure logger.warning fires AT MOST ONCE per session/invocation, not per submit. Provider-unavailable test must run TWO submits and assert one warning.",
    "Narrow exception catch in `_warm_schema_provider`: only `(OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError)` for provider unavailability. Schema violations must propagate from `workflow.validate(...)` and NOT be swallowed by the warmup helper.",
    "Range-check coercion: numeric strings get `float(value)` inside try/except; non-numeric strings simply skip the range check (the runtime accepts widget strings for INT/FLOAT). Do NOT emit errors for non-numeric strings \u2014 that would create false positives.",
    "Link-shape check is intentionally narrow: reject only dict-shaped values on inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. ValidationIssue(\n+                    \"invalid_link_shape\",\n+                    f\"Node {node_id} ({class_type}) input {name} has dict-shaped link; expected [node_id, output_index].\",\n+                    severity=\"error\",\n+                    detail={\n+                        \"node_id\": str(node_id),\n+                        \"class_type\": class_type,\n+                        \"input\": name,\n+                        \"value_repr\": _truncate(value),\n+                    },\n+                )\n+            )\n+    return issues\n+\n+\n def _incoming_inputs(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> dict[str, set[str]]:\n     incoming: dict[str, set[str]] = {}\n     for edge in workflow.edges:\n@@\n def _normalize_type(value: Any) -> str | None:\n     if value is None:\n         return None\n@@\n     if output_type in {\"*\", \"ANY\"} or input_type in {\"*\", \"ANY\"}:\n         return True\n     return False\n+\n+\n+def _is_api_link(value: Any) -> bool:\n+    return (\n+        isinstance(value, (list, tuple))\n+        and len(value) == 2\n+        and isinstance(value[0], str)\n+        and isinstance(value[1], int)\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _truncate(value: Any, n: int = 120) -> str:\n+    text = repr(value)\n+    if len(text) <= n:\n+        return text\n+    return text[: max(0, n - 3)] + \"...\"\n+\n+\n+def _issue_suppressed(class_type: str, code: str) -> bool:\n+    if class_type not in SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES:\n+        return False\n+    return code == \"unknown_input\" or code.startswith(\"value_\")\n+\n+\n+def _schema_accepts_dict(spec: Any) -> bool:\n+    typ = getattr(spec, \"type\", None)\n+    if typ is None:\n+        return False\n+    return str(typ).strip().upper() in {\"DICT\", \"*\"}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/vibecomfy-validator

        Idea:
vibecomfy: validator-pre-submit-gate v1 — promote the existing schema validator into a hard pre-submit gate that runs against the live runtime's `/object_info`, fail loudly with actionable messages, and add the missing checks (link shape, range/enum) that let real bugs through. Do NOT rewrite the materializer.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem

Several "HiddenSwitch incompatibilities" in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` are actually our own materializer/harness bugs that submit invalid graphs the runtime then chokes on:

- ACE materializer emitted dict-shaped links instead of standard Comfy link arrays (`KeyError: 0` in `_compress_graph_nodes`).
- ACE got `bpm=2` (declared min is 10) because we trusted UI widget positions; missing required API fields (`top_p`, `min_p`, `top_k`, `temperature`).
- Both classes are submit-invalid by the runtime's own `/object_info` schema and could be caught up-front.

The infrastructure to catch these already exists but is not wired:

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)` checks unknown class_types, missing required inputs, unknown inputs, edge type mismatches.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py` — `SchemaProvider` fetches `/object_info` and caches it under `vibecomfy/schema/cache.py:object_info_cache_path`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:36` — `ComfyClient.object_info()` already exists.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `VibeWorkflow.validate(schema_provider=None)` exists but the provider parameter is **optional and defaults to None**, so schema checks no-op.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:_prepare_prompt()` (around line 327) calls `workflow.validate()` **with no provider**. The comment explicitly says "Runtime submissions stay structural-only to avoid schema lookup cost on every invocation." — that comment justifies the bug. Caching solves the cost concern.

The validator also misses two checks that would have caught real bugs:
- **Range/enum**: ACE `bpm=2` slipped through because numeric ranges are not enforced.
- **Link shape**: dict-shaped links in API JSON pass structural checks but break the runtime; there is no API-shape sanity check.

# Goal

Make schema validation a hard pre-submit gate. Five mechanical steps:

1. Wire a SchemaProvider into `_prepare_prompt()` and gate submission on `validate(schema_provider=...)`.
2. Add range/enum enforcement to `validate_against_schema`.
3. Add API-link-shape enforcement (every connected input must be `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`, not a dict).
4. Add an opt-out list (`SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`) for known-lying custom-node schemas, with each entry pointing to a `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` row.
5. Make failure messages name the node, class_type, input, and the violated rule — and surface them via the existing CLI surfaces (`vibecomfy run`, `vibecomfy validate`).

Materializer rewrite is explicitly OUT of scope. This is a validation gate only.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — entry point `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:122` — SchemaProvider caches per-server-fingerprint at `object_info_cache_path()`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:148` — first-call fetch is async via `object_info_async()`; subsequent calls are cache-hits.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `validate()` already returns `ValidationReport(ok, issues)`; `ok` is False if any issue has `severity="error"`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` `_prepare_prompt()` already raises `ValueError("Workflow validation failed: ...")` on `not report.ok`. Wiring a provider is sufficient to turn schema checks on.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:20` — `queue_prompt()` posts to `/prompt`; this is the submit boundary.
- `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py` exists as a CLI surface.

# Steps

## S1: Wire SchemaProvider into the submit gate

- In `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`:
  - Construct or accept a `SchemaProvider` for the active runtime URL once per session (not per submit). Store it on the session/runner.
  - Pass it into `workflow.validate(schema_provider=provider)` inside `_prepare_prompt()`.
  - Update the comment that currently justifies skipping schema validation; note that caching makes per-submit cost negligible.
- Default behavior: if `/object_info` cannot be fetched and no cache exists (e.g. fully offline materialization-only path), fall back to structural-only validation with a single WARNING line printed once per session. Never silently skip; never hard-fail when the provider itself is unavailable. Hard-fail only on actual schema violations.

## S2: Range and enum enforcement

- In `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:validate_against_schema`, after the missing/unknown input checks:
  - For each provided input whose schema spec declares numeric `min`/`max`, compare the supplied value (after coercion) and emit `ValidationIssue("value_out_of_range", ...)` on violation.
  - For each input whose schema spec declares an enum/options list, emit `ValidationIssue("value_not_in_enum", ...)` if the value is not a member.
  - Both at `severity="error"`.
- Inspect the existing schema-loading path in `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:_schema_from_object_info` to confirm the spec shape (it likely already carries `min`/`max`/`options` from the raw `/object_info`); if not, extend it minimally to surface them.

## S3: API link-shape enforcement

- Add a new check (can live in `validate_against_schema` or a sibling `validate_api_shape` called from `VibeWorkflow.validate`):
  - For every input on every node, after compilation/edge resolution, the value must be one of: a primitive scalar, a list/tuple, a dict (only when the schema declares dict-shaped input), or an API link in the form `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`.
  - Specifically detect and reject dict-shaped links such as `{"link": ..., "node": ...}` connected to inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Emit `ValidationIssue("invalid_link_shape", ...)`.
- This check runs against the compiled API dict from `VibeWorkflow.compile(backend="api")` if that's the cleanest place; otherwise pre-compile inside `validate()`.

## S4: Per-class skip list for known-lying custom-node schemas

- New module-level constant in `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py`: `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES: dict[str, str]` mapping `class_type` → human-readable reason (and ideally a URL/anchor into `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md`).
- For each class_type in the skip list, suppress only `unknown_input` and `value_*` issues — still enforce `unknown_class_type` and `missing_required_input` (those are usually correct even when widget schemas are wrong).
- Initial entries: empty. Add the first entry only when a real workflow trips a check that is provably the schema's fault, not the workflow's, and add a note in the incompatibilities doc with `Root cause: custom_node_contract`.

## S5: CLI and reporting

- `vibecomfy validate <workflow>` (in `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py`): make schema validation default-on, with `--no-schema` to fall back to structural-only.
- `vibecomfy run <workflow>` and `vibecomfy session ...` paths: the gate is implicit (S1); on failure, print every issue with node id, class_type, input, and the human-readable rule violated, then exit nonzero. Do not print only the first issue.
- Update existing tests under `tests/` that currently submit invalid graphs (if any) to either fix the graph or explicitly call validate-with-skip.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)

- Materializer rewrite. The materializer keeps its current shape; the validator catches its bugs at the boundary. A separate brief will revisit the materializer if the validator surfaces a recurring class of materializer bugs (≥3 times across different workflows).
- Pre-flight doctor that bundles validator + node-presence + model-presence + preview-stripping into a single gating report. Defer.
- Per-workflow input contract / fixture declaration system. Separate brief.
- Auto-repair: the validator only diagnoses, never mutates the graph.
- Type-coercion changes (string vs int widget values). Validator should accept whatever the runtime accepts.

# Design constraints

- **Reversibility**: every behavior change must be toggleable via a single env var or flag (`VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` to disable, or `--no-schema` on the CLI). Do not make the gate non-bypassable.
- **Cache hits**: schema validation must be a cache-hit on every submit after the first per-runtime; first-fetch latency is acceptable.
- **No new top-level deps**.
- **Failure messages must be actionable**: every error names the node id, class_type, the input or rule violated, and the value (truncated if large). No bare "schema check failed".
- **Skip list discipline**: every entry in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` must be cross-referenced from `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` per the Contributing rules in that doc.
- **Do not gate non-submit code paths**: `vibecomfy convert`, `vibecomfy materialize`, etc. should not trigger the gate. Only paths that actually submit to `/prompt` enforce it.

# No prereq clauses

This brief has no hard-halt prereqs. It is independent of the model-registry brief (#5), the watchdog subagent (#2), the override-deletion subagent (#3), and the fixture-library subagent (#4). Ship as soon as ready; do not wait on any of them.

# Validation evidence to produce

- Re-run the previously-failing ACE materialization (the dict-shaped link case and the bpm=2 case): both must now hard-fail at submit with messages that name the node, the class_type, and the rule.
- Re-run the existing 14 runtime-green workflows: all must still pass the gate (no regressions).
- Add unit tests in `tests/test_schema_validate.py` for: missing required input, unknown input, value-out-of-range, value-not-in-enum, invalid-link-shape, skip-list suppression, structural-only fallback when provider is unavailable.

# Open questions

- Should the gate run on the structural workflow object or on the compiled API dict? Both shapes have different bug surfaces (link shape only matters in API form). Lean toward: structural checks on the object, link-shape checks on the compiled API. Validator returns a single combined report.
- Should `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` live in code or in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` as a parsed table? V1: code constant with a docstring pointer to the doc. Parsed-from-doc is a follow-up.
- Cache invalidation: what happens when the runtime upgrades and `/object_info` shape changes? The current cache is fingerprinted by `runtime_fingerprint(server_url)`; confirm that fingerprint includes a runtime version or build hash. If not, that's a separate bug worth flagging but not blocking this brief.

    "id": "T1",
    "description": "In `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py`: (a) add module-level constant `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES: dict[str, str] = {}` with a docstring referencing `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` for entry rules and the suppression contract (only `unknown_input`/`value_*`); (b) extend `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)` so that, after the existing missing/unknown-input loop, for each name in `provided_inputs \u2229 declared_inputs` it looks up the value (`node.inputs[name]` then `node.widgets[name]`), skips when `_is_api_link(value)` returns True, emits `ValidationIssue(\"value_not_in_enum\", ..., severity=\"error\", detail={node_id, class_type, input, value(truncated), choices})` when `spec.choices` is non-empty and the value is missing from it, and emits `ValidationIssue(\"value_out_of_range\", ..., severity=\"error\", detail={node_id, class_type, input, value, min, max})` when `spec.min`/`spec.max` set numeric bounds and the (try/except `float(value)`) coerced value is outside them; non-numeric strings simply skip the range check; (c) add `_is_api_link(value)` returning `isinstance(value, (list,tuple)) and len(value)==2 and isinstance(value[0], str) and isinstance(value[1], int)`; (d) add helper `_truncate(value, n=120)` and use it for any `value` placed in detail dicts; (e) suppress only `unknown_input`, `value_out_of_range`, `value_not_in_enum` for class_types in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` (never `unknown_class_type` or `missing_required_input`); (f) add sibling `validate_api_link_shapes(api_dict, provider)` that iterates each node's `inputs` mapping and for any `dict`-shaped value emits `ValidationIssue(\"invalid_link_shape\", \"Node {node_id} ({class_type}) input {name} has dict-shaped link; expected [node_id, output_index].\", severity=\"error\", detail={node_id, class_type, input, value_repr})` UNLESS the input's schema type is `DICT` or `*`; list/tuple values pass without further checks (do not narrow to canonical `[str,int]`).",
    "depends_on": [],
    "status": "done",
    "executor_notes": "Added empty `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` with docs reference and suppression contract comments. Extended `validate_against_schema` with declared-input enum and numeric min/max checks, canonical API-link `[str, int]` skip, truncated detail values, and skip-list suppression only for `unknown_input`/`value_*`. Added `_is_api_link`, `_truncate`, and `validate_api_link_shapes(api_dict, provider)`, which rejects dict-shaped input values unless the schema input type is `DICT` or `*`; list/tuple values are not narrowed. Verification: `python -m py_compile vibecomfy/schema/validate.py` passed; `PYTHONPATH=. pytest tests/test_schema.py -q` passed (16 passed); ad hoc smoke confirmed `value_out_of_range`, `value_not_in_enum`, `invalid_link_shape`, and selective skip-list suppression.     "Async warmup correctness (FLAG-001): `_prepare_prompt_async` MUST `await provider.object_info_async()` to populate `_object_info` BEFORE calling sync `workflow.validate(schema_provider=...)`. If you call `provider.object_info()` (sync) from inside an async context, `_run_async` raises RuntimeError and the gate silently degrades to structural-only \u2014 the exact bug the gate flagged. Verify with `test_async_warmup_populates_cache_then_validates`.",
    "Submit-order invariant (FLAG-002): the runtime/comfy_server must exist BEFORE the schema provider is built. ServerSession.run was previously calling `_prepare_prompt` before `await self.start()`; runtime/run.py was calling it before entering `comfy_server`. Both reorders are mandatory \u2014 validating against `None` or a different URL than `/prompt` defeats the gate. Verify with `test_server_session_validates_against_started_url` and `test_one_shot_run_validates_against_active_url`.",
    "Embedded path must NOT spawn an extra `comfy_server`: an embedded `Comfy` is already in-process. Use `cache_only=True` and `load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)` directly. If cache is missing, fall back to structural-only with WARNING \u2014 never auto-spawn a second runtime. Verify with `test_embedded_path_does_not_spawn_extra_comfy_server`.",
    "WARNING-once discipline: the `_schema_warning_emitted` flag (or per-invocation `warned` closure in runtime/run.py) must ensure logger.warning fires AT MOST ONCE per session/invocation, not per submit. Provider-unavailable test must run TWO submits and assert one warning.",
    "Narrow exception catch in `_warm_schema_provider`: only `(OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError)` for provider unavailability. Schema violations must propagate from `workflow.validate(...)` and NOT be swallowed by the warmup helper.",
    "Range-check coercion: numeric strings get `float(value)` inside try/except; non-numeric strings simply skip the range check (the runtime accepts widget strings for INT/FLOAT). Do NOT emit errors for non-numeric strings \u2014 that would create false positives.",
    "Link-shape check is intentionally narrow: reject only dict-shaped values on inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Do NOT narrow lists/tuples to canonical `[str,int]` \u2014 too many custom-node inputs accept list literals. The high-value catch is the dict bug (KeyError: 0 in `_compress_graph_nodes`).",
    "Skip-list suppression scope: only suppress `unknown_input`, `value_out_of_range`, `value_not_in_enum` for class_types in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`. NEVER suppress `unknown_class_type` or `missing_required_input` \u2014 those are usually correct even when widget schemas lie.",
    "Reversibility: env var `VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` must short-circuit `_build_schema_provider` to return `None` so the gate is fully bypassed. Test this explicitly with `test_env_var_disables_gate`.",
    "No new top-level deps: do not add anything to `pyproject.toml`. All new code must use stdlib + existing project imports.",
    "Inspect/doctor commands already pass `schema_provider=schema_provider` to `workflow.validate(...)` \u2014 they will silently start emitting new issue codes.         Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/vibecomfy-validator

        Idea:
vibecomfy: validator-pre-submit-gate v1 — promote the existing schema validator into a hard pre-submit gate that runs against the live runtime's `/object_info`, fail loudly with actionable messages, and add the missing checks (link shape, range/enum) that let real bugs through. Do NOT rewrite the materializer.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem

Several "HiddenSwitch incompatibilities" in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` are actually our own materializer/harness bugs that submit invalid graphs the runtime then chokes on:

- ACE materializer emitted dict-shaped links instead of standard Comfy link arrays (`KeyError: 0` in `_compress_graph_nodes`).
- ACE got `bpm=2` (declared min is 10) because we trusted UI widget positions; missing required API fields (`top_p`, `min_p`, `top_k`, `temperature`).
- Both classes are submit-invalid by the runtime's own `/object_info` schema and could be caught up-front.

The infrastructure to catch these already exists but is not wired:

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)` checks unknown class_types, missing required inputs, unknown inputs, edge type mismatches.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py` — `SchemaProvider` fetches `/object_info` and caches it under `vibecomfy/schema/cache.py:object_info_cache_path`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:36` — `ComfyClient.object_info()` already exists.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `VibeWorkflow.validate(schema_provider=None)` exists but the provider parameter is **optional and defaults to None**, so schema checks no-op.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:_prepare_prompt()` (around line 327) calls `workflow.validate()` **with no provider**. The comment explicitly says "Runtime submissions stay structural-only to avoid schema lookup cost on every invocation." — that comment justifies the bug. Caching solves the cost concern.

The validator also misses two checks that would have caught real bugs:
- **Range/enum**: ACE `bpm=2` slipped through because numeric ranges are not enforced.
- **Link shape**: dict-shaped links in API JSON pass structural checks but break the runtime; there is no API-shape sanity check.

# Goal

Make schema validation a hard pre-submit gate. Five mechanical steps:

1. Wire a SchemaProvider into `_prepare_prompt()` and gate submission on `validate(schema_provider=...)`.
2. Add range/enum enforcement to `validate_against_schema`.
3. Add API-link-shape enforcement (every connected input must be `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`, not a dict).
4. Add an opt-out list (`SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`) for known-lying custom-node schemas, with each entry pointing to a `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` row.
5. Make failure messages name the node, class_type, input, and the violated rule — and surface them via the existing CLI surfaces (`vibecomfy run`, `vibecomfy validate`).

Materializer rewrite is explicitly OUT of scope. This is a validation gate only.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — entry point `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:122` — SchemaProvider caches per-server-fingerprint at `object_info_cache_path()`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:148` — first-call fetch is async via `object_info_async()`; subsequent calls are cache-hits.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `validate()` already returns `ValidationReport(ok, issues)`; `ok` is False if any issue has `severity="error"`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` `_prepare_prompt()` already raises `ValueError("Workflow validation failed: ...")` on `not report.ok`. Wiring a provider is sufficient to turn schema checks on.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:20` — `queue_prompt()` posts to `/prompt`; this is the submit boundary.
- `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py` exists as a CLI surface.

# Steps

## S1: Wire SchemaProvider into the submit gate

- In `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`:
  - Construct or accept a `SchemaProvider` for the active runtime URL once per session (not per submit). Store it on the session/runner.
  - Pass it into `workflow.validate(schema_provider=provider)` inside `_prepare_prompt()`.
  - Update the comment that currently justifies skipping schema validation; note that caching makes per-submit cost negligible.
- Default behavior: if `/object_info` cannot be fetched and no cache exists (e.g. fully offline materialization-only path), fall back to structural-only validation with a single WARNING line printed once per session. Never silently skip; never hard-fail when the provider itself is unavailable. Hard-fail only on actual schema violations.

## S2: Range and enum enforcement

- In `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:validate_against_schema`, after the missing/unknown input checks:
  - For each provided input whose schema spec declares numeric `min`/`max`, compare the supplied value (after coercion) and emit `ValidationIssue("value_out_of_range", ...)` on violation.
  - For each input whose schema spec declares an enum/options list, emit `ValidationIssue("value_not_in_enum", ...)` if the value is not a member.
  - Both at `severity="error"`.
- Inspect the existing schema-loading path in `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:_schema_from_object_info` to confirm the spec shape (it likely already carries `min`/`max`/`options` from the raw `/object_info`); if not, extend it minimally to surface them.

## S3: API link-shape enforcement

- Add a new check (can live in `validate_against_schema` or a sibling `validate_api_shape` called from `VibeWorkflow.validate`):
  - For every input on every node, after compilation/edge resolution, the value must be one of: a primitive scalar, a list/tuple, a dict (only when the schema declares dict-shaped input), or an API link in the form `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`.
  - Specifically detect and reject dict-shaped links such as `{"link": ..., "node": ...}` connected to inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Emit `ValidationIssue("invalid_link_shape", ...)`.
- This check runs against the compiled API dict from `VibeWorkflow.compile(backend="api")` if that's the cleanest place; otherwise pre-compile inside `validate()`.

## S4: Per-class skip list for known-lying custom-node schemas

- New module-level constant in `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py`: `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES: dict[str, str]` mapping `class_type` → human-readable reason (and ideally a URL/anchor into `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md`).
- For each class_type in the skip list, suppress only `unknown_input` and `value_*` issues — still enforce `unknown_class_type` and `missing_required_input` (those are usually correct even when widget schemas are wrong).
- Initial entries: empty. Add the first entry only when a real workflow trips a check that is provably the schema's fault, not the workflow's, and add a note in the incompatibilities doc with `Root cause: custom_node_contract`.

## S5: CLI and reporting

- `vibecomfy validate <workflow>` (in `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py`): make schema validation default-on, with `--no-schema` to fall back to structural-only.
- `vibecomfy run <workflow>` and `vibecomfy session ...` paths: the gate is implicit (S1); on failure, print every issue with node id, class_type, input, and the human-readable rule violated, then exit nonzero. Do not print only the first issue.
- Update existing tests under `tests/` that currently submit invalid graphs (if any) to either fix the graph or explicitly call validate-with-skip.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)

- Materializer rewrite. The materializer keeps its current shape; the validator catches its bugs at the boundary. A separate brief will revisit the materializer if the validator surfaces a recurring class of materializer bugs (≥3 times across different workflows).
- Pre-flight doctor that bundles validator + node-presence + model-presence + preview-stripping into a single gating report. Defer.
- Per-workflow input contract / fixture declaration system. Separate brief.
- Auto-repair: the validator only diagnoses, never mutates the graph.
- Type-coercion changes (string vs int widget values). Validator should accept whatever the runtime accepts.

# Design constraints

- **Reversibility**: every behavior change must be toggleable via a single env var or flag (`VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` to disable, or `--no-schema` on the CLI). Do not make the gate non-bypassable.
- **Cache hits**: schema validation must be a cache-hit on every submit after the first per-runtime; first-fetch latency is acceptable.
- **No new top-level deps**.
- **Failure messages must be actionable**: every error names the node id, class_type, the input or rule violated, and the value (truncated if large). No bare "schema check failed".
- **Skip list discipline**: every entry in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` must be cross-referenced from `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` per the Contributing rules in that doc.
- **Do not gate non-submit code paths**: `vibecomfy convert`, `vibecomfy materialize`, etc. should not trigger the gate. Only paths that actually submit to `/prompt` enforce it.

# No prereq clauses

This brief has no hard-halt prereqs. It is independent of the model-registry brief (#5), the watchdog subagent (#2), the override-deletion subagent (#3), and the fixture-library subagent (#4). Ship as soon as ready; do not wait on any of them.

# Validation evidence to produce

- Re-run the previously-failing ACE materialization (the dict-shaped link case and the bpm=2 case): both must now hard-fail at submit with messages that name the node, the class_type, and the rule.
- Re-run the existing 14 runtime-green workflows: all must still pass the gate (no regressions).
- Add unit tests in `tests/test_schema_validate.py` for: missing required input, unknown input, value-out-of-range, value-not-in-enum, invalid-link-shape, skip-list suppression, structural-only fallback when provider is unavailable.

# Open questions

- Should the gate run on the structural workflow object or on the compiled API dict? Both shapes have different bug surfaces (link shape only matters in API form). Lean toward: structural checks on the object, link-shape checks on the compiled API. Validator returns a single combined report.
- Should `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` live in code or in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` as a parsed table? V1: code constant with a docstring pointer to the doc. Parsed-from-doc is a follow-up.
- Cache invalidation: what happens when the runtime upgrades and `/object_info` shape changes? The current cache is fingerprinted by `runtime_fingerprint(server_url)`; confirm that fingerprint includes a runtime version or build hash. If not, that's a separate bug worth flagging but not blocking this brief.

      "id": "T3",
      "description": "Add session-level infrastructure in `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` and a shared formatter: (a) module helpers `_schema_validate_disabled()` returning True iff `os.environ.get(\"VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE\",\"1\").strip() in {\"0\",\"false\",\"False\",\"no\",\"off\"}`, `_build_schema_provider(server_url)` that returns `None` when disabled else `RuntimeSchemaProvider(server_url=server_url)` (lazy import from `vibecomfy.schema`), and `async _warm_schema_provider(provider, *, on_unavailable, cache_only=False)` that returns the provider on success, returns `None` on provider-unavailability after invoking `on_unavailable(msg)`, and ONLY catches `(OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError)` (schema *violations* must surface from `validate`, not be swallowed here). When `cache_only=True`, it consults `load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)` directly and assigns it to `provider._object_info` without entering `comfy_server`; when missing, calls `on_unavailable(...)` and returns `None`. Otherwise it `await provider.object_info_async()`. (b) Add session state `self._schema_provider = None` and `self._schema_warning_emitted = False` in BOTH `EmbeddedSession.__init__` and `ServerSession.__init__`, plus method `_on_schema_unavailable(self, msg)` that logs `logger.warning(\"vibecomfy schema gate: %s\", msg)` exactly once per session. (c) Split `_prepare_prompt`: keep sync `_prepare_prompt(workflow, *, backend, schema_provider=None)` for non-loop callers (CLI/tests) \u2014 calls `workflow.validate(schema_provider=schema_provider)` directly with provider assumed warm or None \u2014 and ADD `async _prepare_prompt_async(workflow, *, backend, schema_provider, on_unavailable, cache_only=False)` that calls `_warm_schema_provider(...)` to get an `effective` provider, runs `report = workflow.validate(schema_provider=effective)`, and on `not report.ok` raises `ValueError(\"Workflow validation failed:\\n  - \" + \"\\n  - \".join(format_issue(i) for i in report.issues if i.severity==\"error\"))`; on success compiles and returns the api dict. Replace the misleading comment at line 360 with `# Schema validation: cache-hit on every submit after the first per-runtime; first-fetch latency acceptable.`. (d) Add a shared formatter: create `vibecomfy/schema/format.py` exposing `format_issue(issue) -> str` with body `d=issue.detail or {}; loc=\" \".join(f\"{k}={d[k]}\" for k in (\"node_id\",\"class_type\",\"input\") if k in d); return f\"[{issue.code}] {loc}: {issue.message}\".strip()` so session and CLI share it.",

(b) `runtime/run.py:run()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:23`): enter `async with comfy_server(server_url=server_url, log_path=log_path) as active_url:` ONCE at the outer level (no double-spawn); inside the context, build `provider = _build_schema_provider(active_url)`, define an inline `warned={\"emitted\":False}` closure for `on_unavailable` that logs warning once, call `api_dict = await _prepare_prompt_async(workflow, backend=backend, schema_provider=provider, on_unavailable=on_unavailable)`, then `await ComfyClient(active_url).queue_prompt(api_dict)`. (c) `EmbeddedSession.run` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:103`): reorder so `await self.start()` runs first; lazily build `self._schema_provider = _build_schema_provider(server_url=None)` once; call `_prepare_prompt_async(..., cache_only=True, on_unavailable=self._on_schema_unavailable)` so warmup uses the on-disk cache via `load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)` and DOES NOT spawn a second `comfy_server`.   "watch_items": [
    "Async warmup correctness (FLAG-001): `_prepare_prompt_async` MUST `await provider.object_info_async()` to populate `_object_info` BEFORE calling sync `workflow.validate(schema_provider=...)`. If you call `provider.object_info()` (sync) from inside an async context, `_run_async` raises RuntimeError and the gate silently degrades to structural-only \u2014 the exact bug the gate flagged. Verify with `test_async_warmup_populates_cache_then_validates`.",
    "Submit-order invariant (FLAG-002): the runtime/comfy_server must exist BEFORE the schema provider is built. ServerSession.run was previously calling `_prepare_prompt` before `await self.start()`; runtime/run.py was calling it before entering `comfy_server`. Both reorders are mandatory \u2014 validating against `None` or a different URL than `/prompt` defeats the gate. Verify with `test_server_session_validates_against_started_url` and `test_one_shot_run_validates_against_active_url`.",
    "Embedded path must NOT spawn an extra `comfy_server`: an embedded `Comfy` is already in-process. Use `cache_only=True` and `load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)` directly. If cache is missing, fall back to structural-only with WARNING \u2014 never auto-spawn a second runtime. Verify with `test_embedded_path_does_not_spawn_extra_comfy_server`.",
    "WARNING-once discipline: the `_schema_warning_emitted` flag (or per-invocation `warned` closure in runtime/run.py) must ensure logger.warning fires AT MOST ONCE per session/invocation, not per submit. Provider-unavailable test must run TWO submits and assert one warning.",
    "Narrow exception catch in `_warm_schema_provider`: only `(OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError)` for provider unavailability. Schema violations must propagate from `workflow.validate(...)` and NOT be swallowed by the warmup helper.",
    "Range-check coercion: numeric strings get `float(value)` inside try/except; non-numeric strings simply skip the range check (the runtime accepts widget strings for INT/FLOAT). Do NOT emit errors for non-numeric strings \u2014 that would create false positives.",
    "Link-shape check is intentionally narrow: reject only dict-shaped values on inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Do NOT narrow lists/tuples to canonical `[str,int]` \u2014 too many custom-node inputs accept list literals. The high-value catch is the dict bug (KeyError: 0 in `_compress_graph_nodes`).",
    "Skip-list suppression scope: only suppress `unknown_input`, `value_out_of_range`, `value_not_in_enum` for class_types in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`. NEVER suppress `unknown_class_type` or `missing_required_input` \u2014 those are usually correct even when widget schemas lie.",
    "Reversibility: env var `VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` must short-circuit `_build_schema_provider` to return `None` so the gate is fully bypassed. Test this explicitly with `test_env_var_disables_gate`.",
    "No new top-level deps: do not add anything to `pyproject.toml`. All new code must use stdlib + existing project imports.",
    "Inspect/doctor commands already pass `schema_provider=schema_provider` to `workflow.validate(...)` \u2014 they will silently start emitting new issue codes. Audit those commands' downstream code paths to ensure they tolerate `value_out_of_range`, `value_not_in_enum`, `invalid_link_shape`, `api_compile_failed`.",
    "Failure messages must list EVERY error issue, not just the first. The `ValueError` message uses `\"\\n  - \".join(...)` over all `severity==\"error\"` issues, formatted by the shared `format_issue` helper.",
    "Cache invalidation under runtime upgrade is acknowledged stale-bug \u2014 `runtime_fingerprint(server_url)` keys remote runtimes only on URL string. NOT in scope here; flag in PR description but do not modify `runtime_fingerprint`.",
    "Sync `_prepare_prompt` is retained for CLI/test use only. After Step 4 reorders, verify via grep that no async path still calls it. Audit is part of T4.",
    "Materializer rewrite is OUT of scope \u2014 validator catches bugs at the boundary; do not touch the ACE materializer in this PR.",
    "Auto-repair is OUT of scope \u2014 validator only diagnoses, never mutates the graph.",
    "Snapshot regression: all 14 runtime-green workflows in `tests/snapshots/*.api.json` must still pass `workflow.validate(schema_provider=...)`. Build a `LocalSchemaProvider` from saved object_info or a minimal class-type-union fixture \u2014 do not require live runtime in CI."

  "meta_commentary": "Execute strictly in order T1\u2192T8 \u2014 each task's invariants depend on its predecessor's output. Two correctness traps to watch (both raised by the gate critique): (1) NEVER call sync `provider.object_info()` from inside an async submit path; it raises via `_run_async` and silently degrades the gate. Always `await provider.object_info_async()` first. (2) NEVER build the schema provider before the runtime URL exists \u2014 both `ServerSession.run` and `runtime/run.py:run()` previously did this; the reorder in T4 is non-negotiable. The embedded path uses `cache_only=True` to avoid spawning a second `comfy_server`; if no cache is on disk, the test (`test_embedded_path_does_not_spawn_extra_comfy_server`) confirms we degrade with a WARNING rather than spinning up another runtime. Keep the warmup-helper exception catch narrow \u2014 `(OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError)` only \u2014 so genuine schema violations from `validate(...)` never get silently swallowed. The skip list (`SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`) starts empty and stays empty in v1; only `unknown_input`/`value_*` issues are suppressible (never `unknown_class_type`/`missing_required_input`). Failure messages must enumerate EVERY error via the shared `format_issue` helper \u2014 no bare \"schema check failed\" string. Cache fingerprint stale-after-upgrade is acknowledged but explicitly out of scope; mention it in the PR description, do not patch `runtime_fingerprint`. Materializer rewrite is OUT of scope \u2014 only the validator changes. The final task (T8) requires writing a short throwaway repro for the two ACE bugs (bpm=2, dict-shaped link), running it to confirm both new error codes fire with actionable messages, then deleting the script \u2014 do not check it in or create new test files for it.",

Runtime submissions stay structural-only to avoid schema lookup cost on every invocation.\n-    report = workflow.validate()\n+def _schema_validate_disabled() -> bool:\n+    return os.environ.get(\"VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE\", \"1\").strip() in {\"0\", \"false\", \"False\", \"no\", \"off\"}\n+\n+\n+def _build_schema_provider(server_url: str | None) -> Any | None:\n+    if _schema_validate_disabled():\n+        return None\n+    from vibecomfy.schema import RuntimeSchemaProvider\n+\n+    return RuntimeSchemaProvider(server_url=server_url)\n+\n+\n+async def _warm_schema_provider(\n+    provider: Any | None,\n+    *,\n+    on_unavailable,\n+    cache_only: bool = False,\n+) -> Any | None:\n+    if provider is None:\n+        return None\n+    try:\n+        if cache_only:\n+            from vibecomfy.schema.cache import load_object_info_cache\n+\n+            cached = load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)\n+            if cached is None:\n+                on_unavailable(f\"object_info cache unavailable at {provider.cache_path}; using structural validation only\")\n+                return None\n+            provider._object_info = cached\n+            return provider\n+\n+        provider._object_info = await provider.object_info_async()\n+        return provider\n+    except (OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError) as exc:\n+        on_unavailable(f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}; using structural validation only\")\n+        return None\n+\n+\n+def _prepare_prompt(\n+    workflow: VibeWorkflow,\n+    *,\n+    backend: str,\n+    schema_provider: Any | None = None,\n+) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    # Schema validation: cache-hit on every submit after the first per-runtime; first-fetch latency acceptable.\n+    report = workflow.validate(schema_provider=schema_provider)\n     if not report.ok:\n-        messages = \"; \".join(issue.message for issue in report.issues)\n-        raise ValueError(f\"Workflow validation failed: {messages}\")\n+        raise ValueError(_validation_failed_message(report))\n \n     try:\n         return workflow.compile(backend=backend)\n@@\n     except Exception as exc:\n         raise RuntimeError(f\"Workflow build failed: {exc}\") from exc\n \n \n+async def _prepare_prompt_async(\n+    workflow: VibeWorkflow,\n+    *,\n+    backend: str,\n+    schema_provider: Any | None,\n+    on_unavailable,\n+    cache_only: bool = False,\n+) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    effective = await _warm_schema_provider(\n+        schema_provider,\n+        on_unavailable=on_unavailable,\n+        cache_only=cache_only,\n+    )\n+    report = workflow.validate(schema_provider=effective)\n+    if not report.ok:\n+        raise ValueError(_validation_failed_message(report))\n+\n+    try:\n+        return workflow.compile(backend=backend)\n+    except ValueError as exc:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Workflow build failed: {exc}\") from exc\n+    except RuntimeError as exc:\n+        raise RuntimeError(f\"Workflow build failed: {exc}\") from exc\n+    except Exception as exc:\n+        raise RuntimeError(f\"Workflow build failed: {exc}\") from exc\n+\n+\n+def _validation_failed_message(report: Any) -> str:\n+    from vibecomfy.schema.format import format_issue\n+\n+    return \"Workflow validation failed:\\n  - \" + \"\\n  - \".join(\n+        format_issue(issue) for issue in report.issues if issue.severity == \"error\"\n+    )\n+\n+\n def _run_metadata(\n*** End Patch\n"

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/vibecomfy-validator

        Idea:
vibecomfy: validator-pre-submit-gate v1 — promote the existing schema validator into a hard pre-submit gate that runs against the live runtime's `/object_info`, fail loudly with actionable messages, and add the missing checks (link shape, range/enum) that let real bugs through. Do NOT rewrite the materializer.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem

Several "HiddenSwitch incompatibilities" in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` are actually our own materializer/harness bugs that submit invalid graphs the runtime then chokes on:

- ACE materializer emitted dict-shaped links instead of standard Comfy link arrays (`KeyError: 0` in `_compress_graph_nodes`).
- ACE got `bpm=2` (declared min is 10) because we trusted UI widget positions; missing required API fields (`top_p`, `min_p`, `top_k`, `temperature`).
- Both classes are submit-invalid by the runtime's own `/object_info` schema and could be caught up-front.

The infrastructure to catch these already exists but is not wired:

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)` checks unknown class_types, missing required inputs, unknown inputs, edge type mismatches.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py` — `SchemaProvider` fetches `/object_info` and caches it under `vibecomfy/schema/cache.py:object_info_cache_path`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:36` — `ComfyClient.object_info()` already exists.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `VibeWorkflow.validate(schema_provider=None)` exists but the provider parameter is **optional and defaults to None**, so schema checks no-op.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:_prepare_prompt()` (around line 327) calls `workflow.validate()` **with no provider**. The comment explicitly says "Runtime submissions stay structural-only to avoid schema lookup cost on every invocation." — that comment justifies the bug. Caching solves the cost concern.

The validator also misses two checks that would have caught real bugs:
- **Range/enum**: ACE `bpm=2` slipped through because numeric ranges are not enforced.
- **Link shape**: dict-shaped links in API JSON pass structural checks but break the runtime; there is no API-shape sanity check.

# Goal

Make schema validation a hard pre-submit gate. Five mechanical steps:

1. Wire a SchemaProvider into `_prepare_prompt()` and gate submission on `validate(schema_provider=...)`.
2. Add range/enum enforcement to `validate_against_schema`.
3. Add API-link-shape enforcement (every connected input must be `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`, not a dict).
4. Add an opt-out list (`SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`) for known-lying custom-node schemas, with each entry pointing to a `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` row.
5. Make failure messages name the node, class_type, input, and the violated rule — and surface them via the existing CLI surfaces (`vibecomfy run`, `vibecomfy validate`).

Materializer rewrite is explicitly OUT of scope. This is a validation gate only.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)

- `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:9` — entry point `validate_against_schema(workflow, provider)`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:122` — SchemaProvider caches per-server-fingerprint at `object_info_cache_path()`.
- `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:148` — first-call fetch is async via `object_info_async()`; subsequent calls are cache-hits.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:155` — `validate()` already returns `ValidationReport(ok, issues)`; `ok` is False if any issue has `severity="error"`.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` `_prepare_prompt()` already raises `ValueError("Workflow validation failed: ...")` on `not report.ok`. Wiring a provider is sufficient to turn schema checks on.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py:20` — `queue_prompt()` posts to `/prompt`; this is the submit boundary.
- `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py` exists as a CLI surface.

# Steps

## S1: Wire SchemaProvider into the submit gate

- In `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`:
  - Construct or accept a `SchemaProvider` for the active runtime URL once per session (not per submit). Store it on the session/runner.
  - Pass it into `workflow.validate(schema_provider=provider)` inside `_prepare_prompt()`.
  - Update the comment that currently justifies skipping schema validation; note that caching makes per-submit cost negligible.
- Default behavior: if `/object_info` cannot be fetched and no cache exists (e.g. fully offline materialization-only path), fall back to structural-only validation with a single WARNING line printed once per session. Never silently skip; never hard-fail when the provider itself is unavailable. Hard-fail only on actual schema violations.

## S2: Range and enum enforcement

- In `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:validate_against_schema`, after the missing/unknown input checks:
  - For each provided input whose schema spec declares numeric `min`/`max`, compare the supplied value (after coercion) and emit `ValidationIssue("value_out_of_range", ...)` on violation.
  - For each input whose schema spec declares an enum/options list, emit `ValidationIssue("value_not_in_enum", ...)` if the value is not a member.
  - Both at `severity="error"`.
- Inspect the existing schema-loading path in `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py:_schema_from_object_info` to confirm the spec shape (it likely already carries `min`/`max`/`options` from the raw `/object_info`); if not, extend it minimally to surface them.

## S3: API link-shape enforcement

- Add a new check (can live in `validate_against_schema` or a sibling `validate_api_shape` called from `VibeWorkflow.validate`):
  - For every input on every node, after compilation/edge resolution, the value must be one of: a primitive scalar, a list/tuple, a dict (only when the schema declares dict-shaped input), or an API link in the form `[node_id_str, output_index_int]`.
  - Specifically detect and reject dict-shaped links such as `{"link": ..., "node": ...}` connected to inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Emit `ValidationIssue("invalid_link_shape", ...)`.
- This check runs against the compiled API dict from `VibeWorkflow.compile(backend="api")` if that's the cleanest place; otherwise pre-compile inside `validate()`.

## S4: Per-class skip list for known-lying custom-node schemas

- New module-level constant in `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py`: `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES: dict[str, str]` mapping `class_type` → human-readable reason (and ideally a URL/anchor into `hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md`).
- For each class_type in the skip list, suppress only `unknown_input` and `value_*` issues — still enforce `unknown_class_type` and `missing_required_input` (those are usually correct even when widget schemas are wrong).
- Initial entries: empty. Add the first entry only when a real workflow trips a check that is provably the schema's fault, not the workflow's, and add a note in the incompatibilities doc with `Root cause: custom_node_contract`.

## S5: CLI and reporting

- `vibecomfy validate <workflow>` (in `vibecomfy/commands/validate.py`): make schema validation default-on, with `--no-schema` to fall back to structural-only.
- `vibecomfy run <workflow>` and `vibecomfy session ...` paths: the gate is implicit (S1); on failure, print every issue with node id, class_type, input, and the human-readable rule violated, then exit nonzero. Do not print only the first issue.
- Update existing tests under `tests/` that currently submit invalid graphs (if any) to either fix the graph or explicitly call validate-with-skip.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)

- Materializer rewrite. The materializer keeps its current shape; the validator catches its bugs at the boundary. A separate brief will revisit the materializer if the validator surfaces a recurring class of materializer bugs (≥3 times across different workflows).
- Pre-flight doctor that bundles validator + node-presence + model-presence + preview-stripping into a single gating report. Defer.
- Per-workflow input contract / fixture declaration system. Separate brief.
- Auto-repair: the validator only diagnoses, never mutates the graph.
- Type-coercion changes (string vs int widget values). Validator should accept whatever the runtime accepts.

# Design constraints

- **Reversibility**: every behavior change must be toggleable via a single env var or flag (`VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` to disable, or `--no-schema` on the CLI). Do not make the gate non-bypassable.
- **Cache hits**: schema validation must be a cache-hit on every submit after the first per-runtime; first-fetch latency is acceptable.
- **No new top-level deps**.
- **Failure messages must be actionable**: every error names the node id, class_type, the input or rule violated, and the value (truncated if large). No bare "schema check failed".
- **Skip list discipline**: every entry in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` must be cross-referenced from `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` per the Contributing rules in that doc.
- **Do not gate non-submit code paths**: `vibecomfy convert`, `vibecomfy materialize`, etc. should not trigger the gate. Only paths that actually submit to `/prompt` enforce it.

# No prereq clauses

This brief has no hard-halt prereqs. It is independent of the model-registry brief (#5), the watchdog subagent (#2), the override-deletion subagent (#3), and the fixture-library subagent (#4). Ship as soon as ready; do not wait on any of them.

# Validation evidence to produce

- Re-run the previously-failing ACE materialization (the dict-shaped link case and the bpm=2 case): both must now hard-fail at submit with messages that name the node, the class_type, and the rule.
- Re-run the existing 14 runtime-green workflows: all must still pass the gate (no regressions).
- Add unit tests in `tests/test_schema_validate.py` for: missing required input, unknown input, value-out-of-range, value-not-in-enum, invalid-link-shape, skip-list suppression, structural-only fallback when provider is unavailable.

# Open questions

- Should the gate run on the structural workflow object or on the compiled API dict? Both shapes have different bug surfaces (link shape only matters in API form). Lean toward: structural checks on the object, link-shape checks on the compiled API. Validator returns a single combined report.
- Should `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES` live in code or in `docs/hiddenswitch_incompatibilities.md` as a parsed table? V1: code constant with a docstring pointer to the doc. Parsed-from-doc is a follow-up.
- Cache invalidation: what happens when the runtime upgrades and `/object_info` shape changes? The current cache is fingerprinted by `runtime_fingerprint(server_url)`; confirm that fingerprint includes a runtime version or build hash. If not, that's a separate bug worth flagging but not blocking this brief.

  "watch_items": [
    "Async warmup correctness (FLAG-001): `_prepare_prompt_async` MUST `await provider.object_info_async()` to populate `_object_info` BEFORE calling sync `workflow.validate(schema_provider=...)`. If you call `provider.object_info()` (sync) from inside an async context, `_run_async` raises RuntimeError and the gate silently degrades to structural-only \u2014 the exact bug the gate flagged. Verify with `test_async_warmup_populates_cache_then_validates`.",
    "Submit-order invariant (FLAG-002): the runtime/comfy_server must exist BEFORE the schema provider is built. ServerSession.run was previously calling `_prepare_prompt` before `await self.start()`; runtime/run.py was calling it before entering `comfy_server`. Both reorders are mandatory \u2014 validating against `None` or a different URL than `/prompt` defeats the gate. Verify with `test_server_session_validates_against_started_url` and `test_one_shot_run_validates_against_active_url`.",
    "Embedded path must NOT spawn an extra `comfy_server`: an embedded `Comfy` is already in-process. Use `cache_only=True` and `load_object_info_cache(provider.cache_path)` directly. If cache is missing, fall back to structural-only with WARNING \u2014 never auto-spawn a second runtime. Verify with `test_embedded_path_does_not_spawn_extra_comfy_server`.",
    "WARNING-once discipline: the `_schema_warning_emitted` flag (or per-invocation `warned` closure in runtime/run.py) must ensure logger.warning fires AT MOST ONCE per session/invocation, not per submit. Provider-unavailable test must run TWO submits and assert one warning.",
    "Narrow exception catch in `_warm_schema_provider`: only `(OSError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError)` for provider unavailability. Schema violations must propagate from `workflow.validate(...)` and NOT be swallowed by the warmup helper.",
    "Range-check coercion: numeric strings get `float(value)` inside try/except; non-numeric strings simply skip the range check (the runtime accepts widget strings for INT/FLOAT). Do NOT emit errors for non-numeric strings \u2014 that would create false positives.",
    "Link-shape check is intentionally narrow: reject only dict-shaped values on inputs whose schema type is not `DICT`/`*`. Do NOT narrow lists/tuples to canonical `[str,int]` \u2014 too many custom-node inputs accept list literals. The high-value catch is the dict bug (KeyError: 0 in `_compress_graph_nodes`).",
    "Skip-list suppression scope: only suppress `unknown_input`, `value_out_of_range`, `value_not_in_enum` for class_types in `SCHEMA_VALIDATION_SKIP_CLASSES`. NEVER suppress `unknown_class_type` or `missing_required_input` \u2014 those are usually correct even when widget schemas lie.",
    "Reversibility: env var `VIBECOMFY_SCHEMA_VALIDATE=0` must short-circuit `_build_schema_provider` to return `None` so the gate is fully bypassed. Test this explicitly with `test_env_var_disables_gate`.",
    "No new top-level deps: do not add anything to `pyproject.toml`. All new code must use stdlib + existing project imports.",
    "Inspect/doctor commands already pass `schema_provider=schema_provider` to `workflow.validate(...)` \u2014 they will silently start emitting new issue codes.
